innōykku mutalāvatākac ceyyavēṇṭiya marut tuvam nilaṅkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷai uḷḷavāṟu cōtittuṇarntu, nilaiyāṉa kuṟainta variyai vitittalākum. kuṟainta vaṭṭikkuk kaṭaṉ koṭuttal, vēḷāṇmai nuṭpaṅkaḷai yāvarum naṉku aṟiyum paṭi ceytal, mutaliyaṉa. aṭuttuc ceytaṟkuriyaṉa. ivai ōraḷavu ceyyappaṭṭuvariṉum, iṉṉum mikutiyākavum viraivākavum ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum. maṟṟu, kalvi kaṟṟup paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷum uḻutoḻil ceyya muṟpaṭṭu, toḻil ceyvataik kuṟaivāka matikkum eṇṇattai māṟṟutal vēṇṭum. ittakaiya ceyalkaḷil aracāṅkattiṉarum nāṭṭu naṉmakkaḷum karuttu vaittāl nāṭu nalam peṟum. 

19. paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camayanilai
tamiḻ makkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē uyir uṭalkaḷai vevvēṟāka uṇarntiruntaṉar eṉpatu tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷukku uyir eṉṟum, mey eṉṟum peyariṭṭirut talāṉē peṟappaṭum. evvaḷavu paḻaṅkālattiṟ ceṉṟu nōkkiṉum tamiḻarkaḷ ulakam, uyir, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum mūṉṟu poruḷkaḷaiyum uṇarntiruntamaiyōṭu, ulakam aimpūtaṅ kaḷālāyateṉṟum, uyirkaḷ viṉaikkaṭṭuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟum, kaṭavuḷ viṉaikkaṭṭillātavar eṉṟum aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. mikka paḻamai vāyntatākiya tol kāppiyattil,
“	kālam ulakam uyirē uṭampē pālvarai teyvam viṉaiyē pūtam”
eṉavum,
“	nilattī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭaintum kalanta mayakkam ulakam”
eṉavum,
“	viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉaivaṉ”
eṉavum kūṟap paṭṭiruttal kāṇka. maṟṟum atilē kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum peyarum, kaṭavuḷai vāḻttutalum kūṟiyiruppa tiṉiṉṟu paramporuḷāṉatu ellāvaṟṟaiyum kaṭantu niṟkum iyalpiṉateṉpatum ataṉai niṉaintu vāḻtti vaḻipaṭutalē uyirkaḷ kaṭṭiṉiṉṟum nīṅki iṉpuṟutaṟkuriya neṟi eṉpatum avarkaḷ aṟittiruntamai pōtarum. iyalpākavē kaṭṭiṉ maiyum, muṟṟuṇarvum, varampiliṉpamum uṭaiya am meyp poruḷaiyē palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil paṟpala tēyattiṉumuḷḷa māntarkaḷ tāmtām aṟintavāṟu vevvēṟu peyarāṉum, vevvēṟu neṟiyāṉum vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻ nāṭṭilē kuṟiñcinila makkaṭku murukak kaṭavuḷum, mullainila makkaṭkut tirumālum, marutanila makkaṭku intiraṉum, neytalnila makkaṭku varuṇaṉum teyvaṅkaḷ eṉat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tiṇaippirivum, tiṇai makkaṭ pirivum iyaṟkaiyāṉavaiyē eṉiṉum, tiṇaikaṭkut teyva aṭaivu ēṟakkuṟaiya nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu ēṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kaṇṇappirāṉ āyarpāṭiyil vaḷarntu āyarkaḷuṭaṉ cērntu āviṉam mēyttal mutaliya viḷaiyāṭalkaḷaik ceytu pōnta piṉṉarē mullai makkaḷiṭattu māyōṉ vaḻipāṭu ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu eṉṉalām. āriya vētaṅkaḷil mikuttuk kūṟap paṭum intiraṉ vaḻipāṭum varuṇaṉ vaḻipāṭum akkālat tiṟṟāṉ tamiḻarkaḷālē kaikkoḷḷap paṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. murukaṉ vaḻipāṭu aṅṅaṉam iṭaiyil uṇṭāyateṉa niṉaikkak kāraṇamillai. iṉi, paṇṭait tamiḻa ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil ciṟappākak kūṟappaṭum kaṭavuḷar nālvar. nakkīrar pāṭiya 56-m puṟappāṭṭilē iṭapakkoṭiyum kaṇiccip paṭaiyum maṇimiṭaṟum uṭaiya civaperumāṉum, vālvaḷai mēṉiyum kalappaip paṭaiyum paṉaikkoṭiyum uṭaiya palarāmaṉum, nīlamaṇi yokkum mēṉiyum kaluḻakkoṭiyum uṭaiya kaṇṇaṉum, mayilākiya koṭiyum mayilūrtiyum uṭaiya murukavēḷum muṟaiyē kūṟappeṟṟuḷar. atil,
“	ñālaṅ kākkum kāla muṉpiṉ tōlā nallicai nālvar”
eṉa ivarkaḷ ulakattaip purappōrākak kūṟiyiruppatum cintikkaṟpālatu. kapilar pāṭiya iṉṉānāṟpatilum,
“	mukkaṭ pakavaṉ aṭitoḻā tārkkiṉṉā poṟpaṉai veḷḷaiyai uḷḷā toḻukiṉṉā cakkarat tāṉai maṟappiṉṉā āṅkiṉṉā cattiyāṉ ṟāṭoḻā tārkku”
eṉa innālvarum immuṟaiyē kūṟappeṟṟaṉar. cilappatikāram intira viḻavūreṭutta kātaiyil,
“	piṟavā yākkaip periyōṉ kōyilum aṟumukac cevvēḷ aṇitikaḻ kōyilum vālvaḷai mēṉi vāliyōṉ kōyilum nīlamēṉi neṭiyōṉ kōyilum”
eṉavum, ūrkāṇ kātaiyil,
“	nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟaiyōṉ kōyilum uvaṇac cēval uyarttōṉ niyamamum mēḻivalaṉ uyartta veḷḷai nakaramum kōḻic cēvaṟ koṭiyōṉ kōṭṭamum”
eṉavum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattilum, maturaiyilum innāṉku tēvarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉavāka muṟaiyē kūṟap peṟṟuḷa. ivaṟṟil nālvariṉ muṟai muṟkūṟiya vāṟaṉṟic ciṟitu piṟaḻntuḷatu. eṉiṉum, yāṇṭum civaperumāṉē mutalil vaittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar aṉṟiyum. avar piṟavā yākkaip periyōṉ eṉavum pakavaṉ eṉavum, iṟaiyōṉ eṉavum, talaimai tōṉṟak kūṟappaṭutalum cintikkaṟpālatu. vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil iṉṉum veḷippaṭaiyākavum civapirāṉukku mutaṉmai kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu. 
“	nīrum nilaṉum tīyum vaḷiyum māka vicumpō ṭaintuṭa ṉiyaṟṟiya  maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ talai vaṉāka mācaṟa viḷaṅkiya yākkaiyar”
eṉa māṅkuṭi marutaṉār ‘maturaik kāñci yilum,
“	nutalviḻi nāṭṭan tiṟaiyōṉ mutalāp pativāḻ catukkattut teyvamī ṟāka”
eṉak kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār maṇimēkalaiyilum kūṟi yiruttal nōkkuka. ivvāṟṟāl, toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻ makkaḷ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vanta teyvam civaperumāṉē eṉpatu veḷḷiṭai malaipōl teḷḷitiṉ viḷaṅkum. piṟ kālattuc camaṇavāciriyar mutalāyiṉārum tamiḻ vaḻakkiṉ ivvuṇmai yuṇarntē tam nūlkaḷil pala kaṭavuḷaraiyum kūṟumiṭattuc civaperumāṉai mutaṟ kaṇ vaittuk kūṟip pōntaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, aṭiyārkku nallār mutaliya uraiyāciriyaṉmārum .inneṟi piḻaiyāmalē urai varaivārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷ tirumāliṭattum cevvēḷiṭattum niṟainta aṉpuṭaiyarākavē viḷaṅkiṉar. iṉṉōraiyum ulakaṅkākkum kaṭavuḷarākap pōṟṟip pukaḻntaṉarēyaṉṟi oru ciṟitum kuṟaivupaṭak kūṟiṉārallar. maṟṟum tokai nūl kaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttilum, paripāṭaliṉum civapirāṉōṭu vēṟṟumaiyillāta mutaṟ ṟeyvaṅkaḷākavē ivarkaḷ paravappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉum, civa vaḻipāṭṭiṉum cevvēḷ vaḻipāṭu viñciyiruntateṉak kūṟutalum cālum. pattup pāṭṭil oru pāṭṭum (tirumurukāṟṟup paṭaiyum) paripāṭaliṉ muppattoru pāṭṭum murukappirāṉukku uriyavāyiruttalē ivvuṇmaiyai viḷakkum; murukavēḷ eḻuntaruḷi yirukkum tiruppatikaḷ pala caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ payiṉṟiruttalum ataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. tirupparaṅkuṉṟam, tiruccīralaivāy, tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭi, tiruvērakam mutaliya tiruppatikaḷait tirumurukāṟṟup paṭai kūṟukiṟatu. paripāṭalum, piṟa tokai kaḷum avaṟṟaip pukaḻnturaikkiṉṟaṉa; cilappatikāramum,
“	cīrkeḻu centilum *ceṅkōṭum veṇkuṉṟum ērakamum nīṅkā iṟaivaṉ”
eṉṟēttukiṉṟatu. 
iṉi, vaṭa nāṭṭiṟ ceṅkōlōcciya peruvēnta ṉākiya acōkaṉ taṉ makkaḷākiya caṅkamittirai, makēntu eṉpavarkaḷuṭaṉ puttamata pōtakar palarait tēyantōṟum viṭuttu ammatattaip paravac ceytamaiyāl, ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē pauttamatam tamiḻ nāṭṭilum iṭam peṟṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. camaṇa matamum akkālan toṭaṅkiyē paravaluṟṟatu eṉṉalām. cilappatikāram mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu nōkkumiṭattu, ki.pi. mutal, iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilē, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ talainakaraṅkaḷākiya pukār, uṟantai, maturai, vañci, kāñci eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil camaṇa pautta mataṅkaḷ mikka valimaiyuṟṟiruntamai pulaṉākiṉṟatu. arukaṉ kōṭṭamum, puttacaittiyamum nakarppuṟaṅkaḷil kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa; camaṇa muṉivarkaḷum, pautta muṉivar kaḷum uṟaiyum paḷḷikaḷ āṅkāṅkiruntaṉa; cāraṇarkaḷ iṭaiyaṟātu matapōtaṉai purintu vantārkaḷ; āṭavarkaḷum peṇṭirkaḷum mikutiyāka ammataṅkaḷiṟ pukuvārāyiṉar. akkālattut tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ naṭuvunilaiyuṭaṉ it taṉaikkum iṭantantu avarkaḷaiyum purantu vantamai peritum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. civaṉ, tirumāl mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷai eḷḷutaliṉṟiyē avarkaḷ tam mataṅkaḷaip pōtittu vantamaiyāṟṟāṉ avai viraintu paravaluṟṟaṉa eṉak karutal poruntum.
iṉi, akkālattiluḷḷa pulavarkaḷāyiṉum piṟarāyiṉum avarkaḷ tammiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa camayattārkaḷiṉ uṇarcciyai matittu avaṟṟaicciṟitum ikaḻātu pārāṭṭi vantaṉar eṉpatum, ōr kuṭumpattiluḷḷa palar tattam viruppattiṟ kiyaintavāṟu vevvēṟu mataṅkaḷaik kaikkoṇṭum tammuḷ vēṟṟumai ciṟitumiṉṟi oḻuki vantaṉar eṉpatum paḻaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum aṟiyalākiṉṟa uṇmaikaḷām. ec camayattaiyum ikaḻātu pōṟṟum peruntaṉmaiyuṭaimaikkut teyvap pulamait tiruvaḷḷuvaṉāraiyum, iḷaṅkōvaṭi kaḷaiyum ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭākak koḷḷal takum.
vaḷḷuvaṉār,
“	nulōr tokuttavaṟṟu ḷellām talai”
“	pallāṟṟāl tēriṉu maḵtē tuṇai”
eṉṟiṅṅaṉam ellā nūliṉum nallaṉa eṭuttu ellārkkum potuppaṭak kūṟic celvar; em matattaiyum ikaḻār.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, tam tamaiyaṉākiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ civaperumāṉ tiruvaruḷāṟ piṟantavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ imayañ cellap puṟappaṭṭa poḻutu ulaku potiyuruvat tuyarn tōṉākiya civaperumāṉ cēvaṭiyai muṭimēṟ koṇṭu yāraiyum iṟaiñcāc ceṉṉiyāl iṟaiñci valaṅkoṇṭu pōntu yāṉaiyiṉ piṭaril ēṟiyaruḷiya piṉpu āṭakamāṭattu aṟituyilamarnta tirumāliṉ cēṭaṅ koṇṭu vantu cilar niṉṟu tutittakālaiyil tāṉ civaperumāṉ tiruvaṭiyai maṇimuṭi mēl vaittiruttaliṉ ataṉai vāṅkit tōḷiṉ mītu tarittaṉaṉ eṉṟum kūṟu mukattāl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ civaperumāṉaiyē muḻu mutalākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭuvōṉ eṉpatu peṟa vaittu, tam koḷkaiyum atuvātalai ‘piṟavāyāk kaip periyōṉ’ eṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ kuṟippiṭṭaṉar. aṉṉarāyiṉum, avar, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ arukatēvaṉai vāḻttutal kūṟumiṭattu,
“	orumūṉ ṟavittōṉ ōtiya ñāṉat tirumoḻik kallateṉ ceviyakam tiṟavā kāmaṉai veṉṟōṉ āyiratteṭṭu nāmam allatu navilā teṉṉā aivarai veṉṟōṉ aṭiyiṇai yallatu kaivaraik kāṇiṉum kāṇā eṉkaṇ aruḷaṟam pūṇṭōṉ tirumeyk kallateṉ poruḷil yākkai pūmiyiṟ poruntātu”
eṉṟiṅṅaṉamum;
vēṭṭuva variyil avarkaḷ koṟṟavaiyaip paravu miṭattu,
“	amari kumari kavuri camari cūli nīli mālavaṟ kiḷaṅkiḷai aiyai ceyyavaḷ veyyavāṭ ṭaṭakkaip pāykalaip pāvai paintoṭip pāvai āykalaip pāvai aruṅkalappāvai”
“	āṉaittōl pōrttup puliyi ṉuriyuṭuttuk kāṉat terumaik karuntalaimēl niṉṟāyāl vāṉōr vaṇaṅka maṟaimēṉ maṟaiyāki ñāṉak koḻuntāy naṭukkiṉṟi yēniṟpāy”
eṉṟu ivvāṟākavum;
āycciyar kuravaiyil avarkaḷ tirumālaip paravu miṭattu,
“	periyavaṉai māyavaṉaip pērulaka mellām virikamala vuntiyuṭai viṇṇavaṉaik kaṇṇum tiruvaṭiyum kaiyum kaṉivāyum ceyya kariyavaṉaik kāṇāta kaṇṇeṉṉa kaṇṇe kaṇṇimaittuk kāṇpārtam kaṇṇeṉṉa kaṇṇē”
eṉṟu ittaṉmaiyākavum:
kuṉṟak kuravaiyil kuṟavarkaḷ murukavēḷaip pāṭu miṭattu, 
“	cīrkeḻu centilum ceṅkōṭum veṇkuṉṟum ērakamum nīṅkā iṟaivaṉkai vēlaṉṟē pārirum pauvatti ṉuḷpukkup paṇṭorunāḷ cūrmā taṭinta cuṭarilaiya veḷvēlē”
eṉṟu iṉṉaṇamum; avaravar uṇarcciyuṭaṉ kalantu ellāt teyvaṅkaḷaiyum vāyāra maṉamāra vaḻuttuvārāyiṉar.
iṉi, civapirāṉ tirukkōyil mutalāka muṉ kūṟip pōnta kōyilkaḷaṉṟi, mācāttaṉ, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ eṉṉum teyvaṅkaṭkum, tirukkailai, cevvēḷiṉ kaivēl mutaliya vaṟṟukkum taṉittaṉiyāka iyaṟṟappeṟṟa kōyilkaḷ annāḷilē nilaviṉaveṉka.

20. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum iyaṟkaippuṉaivum
ulakamum ulakattil uḷḷaṉavumākiya poruḷkaḷai yellām iyaṟkaip poruḷ, ceyaṟkaip poruḷ eṉa iruvakaiyākap pākupāṭu ceytal kūṭum. nam camaya nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi niṉṟu nōkkiṉ, aimpūtaṅkaḷum iyaṟkaip poru ḷeṉavum, avaṟṟiṉ kalappālākiya ulakamum ulakattup poruḷkaḷum ceyaṟkaip poruḷeṉavum tōṉṟum.
“	nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭaintum kalanta mayakkam ulaka mātaliṉ”
eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum kūṟiṉar. iṉṉum nuṇṇitiṉ nōkkiṉ māyaiyiṉ kāriyamākum aimpūtam uḷḷiṭṭa aṉaittumē ceyaṟkaip poruḷeṉal tōṉṟum. navīṉa aṟiviyaṟkalaikaḷeṉappaṭum caṭatattuva nūlkaḷ kūṟum iyaṟkaip poruḷ ceyaṟkaip poruḷ eṉpaṉa vēṟu vakaiyiṉa. ivviru vakaiyiṉum vēṟupaṭṭaṉa kavivāṇar koḷḷum iyaṟkaip poruḷ ceyaṟkaip poruḷ eṉpaṉa,
kaviñarulakilē, makkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭātu kaṭavuḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavō, iyaṟkaiyil amaintiruppaṉavō ākiya malai, kaṭal, nāṭu, kāṭu, eṉpaṉavum, avaṟṟiṭai iyalpil uṇṭāvaṉavum ākiyavai iyaṟkaip poruḷ eṉavum makkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭum aṟucuvaiyuṇṭi, eḻunilai māṭam, paṭṭāṭai, maṇikkalaṉ, mutaliya yāvum ceyaṟkaip poruḷ eṉavum koḷḷappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatākiya iyaṟkaip poruḷaiyē Nature eṉṟu  āṅkilattiṟ kūṟuvar. makkaḷ tam aṟivāṟṟalkaḷāl evvaḷavu ariya poruḷkaḷai ākkik koḷḷiṉum avaiyellām iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷpōl uṭal vaḷamum maṉa makiḻvum taraṟpālaṉavalla. ivvuṇmaiyai aṟintē nam muṉṉōrkaḷ tam vāḻkkaiyaip peritum iyaṟkaiyai oṭṭi naṭatti vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭaik kālattilirunta tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ iyaṟkaiyiṉ perumaiyai naṉkaṟintavarkaḷ eṉpataṟkut tamiḻiṉ tiṇai vakuppukaḷē cāṉṟākum.
malaiyum ataṉaic cārnta iṭamum kuṟiñciyeṉavum, puṟavu allatu kāṭu mullaiyeṉavum, nīr vaḷamuṭaiya paḻaṉam mutaliyaṉa marutam eṉavum, kaṭal cārnta nilam neytal eṉavum tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟum. vaḷañciṟitumillāta vaṟaṇṭa curam pālai yeṉappaṭum. avvan nilattil uṟaiyum makkaḷ, talaimakkaḷ, avarkaḷ vaḻipaṭum teyvam, avarkaṭkuriya uṇavu, toḻil, yāḻ, aṅkuḷḷa vilaṅku, paṟavai,maram,pū mutaliyaṉavellām karupporuḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum iṉṉa nilattil iṉṉavai uḷavākum eṉṉum iyaṟkai yuṇmai yaip paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷ aṟintāṟ pōla vēṟu yārum aṟintilareṉṟē kūṟalām. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷākiya eṭṭut tokai pattup pāṭṭu eṉpavaṟṟai iyaṟkaiyiṉ ōviyaṅkaḷ eṉṉalām. avaṟṟiluḷḷa ovvoru pāṭṭum iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷai nam akak kaṇmuṉ tōṟṟuvittu iṉpam viḷaikkiṉṟaṉa. utāraṇamāka oṉṟu kāṭṭutum:
‘kuṟiñci nilattiluḷḷa oru talaiviyum tōḻiyum, tiṉaippuṉam kāttaṟkuc ceṉṟu, uyarnta marattiṉmītu  iṭappaṭṭirukkum paraṇiṉ kaṇ ēṟiyiruntu, taṭṭai. mutaliya karuvikaḷāl kiḷikaḷai yōṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. ñāyiṟu kāyum naṇpakaṟ poḻutil paṟavaikaḷ tattam cēkkaikaḷai aṭai kiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutu avviruvarum aṅku niṉṟakaṉṟu, malaiyucciyiṉiṉṟum tūya veḷḷāṭai pōṟ parantu iḻiyum aruviyil nīrāṭukiṉṟaṉar. paḷiṅkaik karaittu vaittāṟ pōṉṟa kaṉaikaḷilē iṉiya pala pāṭṭukkaḷaip pāṭik koṇṭu kuṭaintu kuṭaintu viḷaiyāṭukiṉṟaṉar; piṉ karaiyēṟi, poṉṉilē nīlamaṇiyaip patittu vaittāṟ pōl mutukilē tāḻnta piṉṉalākiya kūntalaip piḻintu īram pularttiviṭṭu, civanta kaṇkaḷuṭaṉ pala pakkamum tirintu malar koykiṉṟaṉar. ceṅkāntaḷ, cuṉaikkuvaḷai, kuṟiñci, veṭci, eruvai, kuravam, ceruviṉai, karuviṉai, kōṅkam, pōṅkam, cerunti, karantai, tillai, mullai, pātiri, caṇpakam, mutaliya nūṟuvakaiyāṉa pūkkaḷaip paṟittu vantu akaṉṟa pāṟaiyilē kuvittu, avaṟṟāl taḻai eṉṉum uṭaiyiṉaic ceytu iṭaiyil uṭuttiyum, palvēṟu uruviṉaiyuṭaiya mālai kaḷaip puṉaintu cūṭiyum,. neruppeṉa viḷaṅkun taḷirkaḷai yuṭaiya acōka marattiṉ kuḷirnta niḻalil iṉitu vīṟṟiruk kiṉṟaṉar.’ ivviyaṟkaip puṉaivu kapilar pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pāṭṭil uḷḷatu. kōtāvariyāṟṟukkuc cāṉṟōr kaviyiṉai uvamai kūṟiya kampar iraṇṭaṟkum potuvāka, “aintiṇai neṟiyaḷāvi” eṉṟuraittār. eṉavē kavitaikaṭku vaṉapput taruvaṉa aintiṇaip pāṟpaṭum iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷām eṉpatu pōtarum.
iṉi, peruṅ kāppiyaṅkaṭku ivviyaṟkaip puṉaivukaḷ iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa veṉpatu, “malaikaṭal nāṭu vaḷanakar paruvam, irucuṭart tōṟṟa meṉṟiṉaiyaṉa puṉaintu” eṉa ataṟkilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvataṉāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻiluḷḷa peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷil kālattāl muṟpaṭṭatum, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ iyalpaiyum tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ perumaiyaiyum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiruppatum muttamiḻk kāppiyamākiya cilappatikāram ākum. ataṉai iyaṟṟiyavar ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum cēra vēntaṟkut tampiyārum tuṟavu pūṇṭirunta varumākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ āvar. muttamiḻt tuṟaiyiṉ muṟai pōkiya uttamak kaviyākum iḷaṅkōviṉ kāppiyat til ettaṉaiyō pala vittaka cittiraṅkaḷ viyappum uvappum viḷaikka vallaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ iyaṟkaip poruḷiṉ puṉaivāka vuḷḷa cilavaṟṟai īṇṭu nōkkutum.
cilappatikārattiṉ muppatu kātaikaḷil ‘nāṭu kāṇ kātai’ eṉpatoṉṟu. kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuk kāviriyiṉ vaṭakarai vaḻiyākak kuṭaticai nōkki vantu tiruvaraṅkattiṟku etirē oṭam ēṟit teṉkaraiyeyti uraiyūrai aṭaintaṉar eṉṉum in nikaḻcciyait terivikkum pakutikku nāṭu kāṇ kātai eṉṟu peyariṭṭuḷḷa nuṭpam aṟintu makiḻtaṟkuriyatu.
“	nāṭeṉpa nāṭā vaḷattaṉa nāṭalla nāṭa vaḷantaru nāṭu”
eṉpatu teyvappulavariṉ meyyuraiyaṉṟō? vaḷam mikka nilappakutiyai āḷutaliṉālēyē vaḷavar eṉap peyar peṟṟa cōḻaratu nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ avvaḷattiṟkuk kāraṇamākiya kāviriyiṉ maruṅkuḷḷa nilam ettuṇai vaḷamikkatākal vēṇṭum? avvaḷamellām avarkaṭkuk kāṭcip poruḷāki iṉpam viḷaittaṉa eṉṉuṅ karuttut tōṉṟavē aṭikaḷ ‘nāṭukaṭanta kātai’ eṉṉātu ‘nāṭukāṇ kātai’ eṉap peyariṭṭār eṉka. kōvalaṉ kaṇṇaki iruvarum camaṇ camayattavamutiyōḷākiya kavunti irukkumiṭam aṭaintaṉar. kavuntiyaṭikaḷum avar kaḷuṭaṉ maturaikkuvara oruppaṭṭu kāvirimaruṅkuḷḷa en neṟiyil evvāṟu tām cella vēṇṭumeṉpatai avar kaṭkuk kūṟukiṉṟavar, “veyiliṉ vemmaiyaip poṟukka lāṟṟāta meṉmaiyaiyuṭaiya kaṇṇakiyaip pūkkaḷ niṟainta cōlaikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē aḻaittuc celvēmāyiṉ kiḻaṅkukaḷ tōṇṭiyeṭutta kuḻikaḷaic caṇpakattiṉ pūntukaḷum, paḻam pūkkaḷum viḻuntu mūṭi poykkuḻippaṭuttalāl avaṟṟai aṟintu vilakic cellātavarkaṭku avai mikka tuṉpattiṉait tarā niṟkum. avvāṟu vilakic cellum poḻutu mēlē toṅkum palāppaḻam muṭṭiṉum muṭṭum. mañcaḷum iñciyum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu piṉṉikkiṭakkum pāttikaḷiluḷḷa palāviṉ vittukkaḷ paralpōlat tuṉpuṟuttaluṅkūṭum. taṇṭalai vaḻiyiṟcellātu vayal vaḻiyē pōvōm eṉṟāl, āṅkaṇ poykaiyiluḷḷa kayal mīṉkaḷai ōṭṭi nīrnāy kavviya vāḷaikaḷ malaṅkukaḷ piṟaḻum ceṟuvilē kuṟukkākap pāyiṉ ivaḷ kalaṅkaluṅkūṭum. vayalum cōlaiyumallatu vēṟākiya vaḻiyumillai; ākaliṉ avvaḻikaḷil kaṇṇakikku iṉṉal uṇṭākātu pātukāttu varuka” eṉṟu kūṟi avarum uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. mūvarum celvuḻi., kāviriyiṉ nīr vāyttalaik kiṭṭa kataviṉ mīteḻuntu kutikkum oliyaṉṟi, āmpi, kiḻār, ēttam mutaliya nīr iṟaikkum poṟikaḷ olittal illāta kaḻaṉikaḷilē cennelum karumpum cūḻnta paḻaṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tāmaraip pūkkaḷākiya kāṭṭil,
“	kampuṭ kōḻiyum kaṉaikural nāraiyum, ceṅkāl aṉṉamum paiṅkāṟ kokkum,  kāṉak kōḻiyum, nīr niṟakkākkaiyum, uḷḷum ūralum puḷḷum putāvum”
eṉṉum paṟavaikaḷ vēntariruvar pōr ceyyumiṭattil aṇivakuttu niṟkum paṭaikaḷ pōl nirainiraiyāyiruntu eḻuppum palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa olikaḷum eḻukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟum, cēṟṟiṉiṟ puraṇṭu kaḻuvappaṭāta uṭampiṉaiyuṭaiya erumai kaḷ tiṉavuṭaiya mutukiṉait tēyttalāl purinetiḻntu neṟ kūṭṭiluḷḷa palavakai neṟkaḷum arukē kaḻaṉikaḷiṟ kavaripōṟ kāytta cen neṟkatirkaḷiṉ mītē coriyā niṟka ataṉaik kaṇṭa viṉaiñarum, kaḷamarum,oruṅku kūṭi ārkkum oli orupuṟamum, kaṭaiciyar tāmarai āmpal mutaliya kaḷai kaḷaip paṟit teṟintu nāṟṟiṉai naṭṭut tōḷum mārpum cēṟāṭiya kōlattuṭaṉ vīṟupeṟat tōṉṟi matuvuṇṭu pāṭum pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ oli oru puṟamum, aṟukaiyum kuvaḷai malaraiyum katiruṭaṉ kalantu toṭutta mālaiyai, mēḻiyilē cūṭṭi nilattiṉaip piḷappavar pōl poṉṉēr pūṭṭi niṉṟōr pāṭum ēr maṅkalappāṭṭiṉ ocai oru puṟamum, neṟ katirai arintu pōriṭṭōr ataṉaik kaṭāviṭum poḻutu pāṭum mukavaip pāṭṭiṉ ocai oru puṟamum eḻāniṉṟaṉa, kāviriyiṉ aṭai karaiyiṟ cellum kōvalaṉum, kaṇṇa kiyum ippalavakai yolikaḷaiyum muṟaiyiṟkēṭṭu maṉaṅ kaḷittalāl vaḻi naṭattaliṉuṇṭām varuttattai neñciṟ koḷḷātu celvārāyiṉar. cōṇāṭṭiṉ iyaṟkai vaḷattiṉai iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ivvāṟu niralpaṭak kūṟi yirukkun tiṟaṉ kaṟpārkkuk kaḻipēṟuvakai payappatākum.
iṉi, ivarkaḷ vaiyai yāṟṟaik kaṭantu maturaikkuc cellutal kūṟum poḻutu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ vaiyaiyai ōr naṅkaiyāka uruvakappaṭutti ‘pulavar nāviṟ poruntiya pūṅkoṭi’ eṉavum, ‘vaiyaiyeṉṟa poyyāk kulakkoṭi’ eṉavum arumai pārāṭṭukiṉṟavar, ataṟkēṟpa, ataṉaic cārntuḷḷa iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷai an naṅkaiyiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷākap puṉainturaikkiṉṟaṉar. vaiyaiyiṉ karai maruṅkulāka, ataṉ puṟavāyil aṭarnta kuravam, vakuḷam, kōṅkam, vēṅkai, maravam, nākam, tilakam, marutam, cēṭal, cerunti,ceṇpakam, pātiriyākiya maraṅkaḷ malar virintu  viḷaṅkuvaṉa pūntukilākavum, ataṉ akavāyiluḷḷa kuruku, taḷavam, mucuṇṭai, atiral,kūtāḷam, kuṭacam, vetiram, pakaṉṟai, piṭavam, mayilai eṉṉum tūṟukaḷum, koṭikaḷum malarntu piṉṉikkiṭappaṉa mēkalaiyākavum aḻaku cey kiṉṟaṉavām! vaiyaiyāṟu muḻutum nīr puṟantōṉṟāmal malarkaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭiruppatu avaḷ kaṇṇakikku mēl varuntuṉpattai aṟintu ‘puṇṇiya naṟumalar āṭai pōrttuk, kaṇṇiṟai neṭunīr karantaṭakkiyatu’ pōlkiṉṟa tām! ivaṟṟiliruntu iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷiṉ kāṭciyil iḷaṅkōviṉ uḷḷam evvāṟu īṭupaṭṭirunta teṉpatum tām kaṇṭu nukarnta apporuḷkaḷaik kataiyil eṅṅaṉam ēṟṟa peṟṟiyamaittuc cuvai yūṭṭukiṉṟaṉareṉpatum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, cilappatikārattil kāṭcik kātai eṉpa toṉṟuṇṭu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṇṇakikkup paṭimañ ceytaṟ poruṭṭu imaya malaiyiṟ kaṟkoḷḷak karutiṉaṉ eṉpatu akkātaiyiṉ muṭinta poruḷāyiṉum, kuṟiñci nilattiṉ iyaṟkai vaḷaṅkaḷai avaṉ kaṇṭu kaḷittamai ataṉ muṟpakutiyākavuḷḷatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ mañcucūḻ cōlai malaivaḷaṅ kāṇṭaṟ poruṭṭāka taṉ tēviyākiya vēṇ māḷuṭaṉum, viḷaiyāṭṭiṟkuriya palavakaip poruḷuṅ koṇṭu tāṉaikaḷ cūḻa vañci nakariṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟu pērāṟṟiṉ karaiyai aṭaikiṉṟāṉ. avviyāṟāṉatu ‘kōṅkam vēṅkai tūṅkiṇark koṉṟai, nākam tilakam naṟuṅkāḻāram’ ākiya palavaṟṟiṉiṉṟum utirnta pūkkaḷiṉ parappiṉāl puṉal tōṉṟātu maṟaiya, neṭiyōṉ mārpil ārampōṉṟu periya malaiyaik kuṟukkiṭṭuc cellā niṉṟatu. ataṉ aṭaikaraiyiluḷḷa maṇal ekkaril aracaṉ mutalāṉōr ceṉṟu oruṅkirukkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkē kuṉṟak kuṟavarkaḷiṉ kuravaiyiṟkoṭicciyar pāṭum pāṭṭiṉō caiyum, cevvēḷaip pūcikkum vēlaṉatu pāṭṭiṉ ocaiyum, tiṉai kuṟṟuvōratu vaḷḷaip pāṭṭōcaiyum puṉattilē kiḷiyōṭṭutal mutaliya vaṟṟāṉeḻum ōcaiyum, iṟāli ṉiṉṟum tēṉeṭukkum kuṟavarōcaiyum muḻaveṉa olikkum aruviyiṉ ōcaiyum, puliyoṭu pōr ceyyum yāṉaiyiṉ ocaiyum,  paraṇiṉ mēluḷḷavaṉ vilaṅkiṉaik kaṭintōṭṭum ōcaiyum, kuḻiyil viḻukiṉṟa yāṉaiyiṉ pākar ceyyum ōcaiyum, iyaṅkukiṉṟa paṭaikaḷiṉ ōcaiyōṭu oruṅkeḻuvaṉa vāyiṉa. avvōcai yiṉpattai nukarntirukkum poḻutu vēṅkai marattiṉ niḻalil kaṇṇaki kōvalaṉōṭu tēva vimāṉattil ēṟic ceṉṟa aticayattaik kaṇṭa kuṉṟak kuṟavar kaḷ ataṉait taṅkaḷ malaināṭṭukku vēntaṉākiya ceṅkuṭṭu vaṟku aṟivikkak karutip pala kāṇikkaip poruḷkaḷōṭum vantu aracaṉaik kaṇṭu terivikkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ koṇarnta poruḷkaḷ ivaiyeṉak kūṟumvāyilāka iḷaṅ kōvaṭikaḷ malaivaḷamaṉaittaiyum oruṅku tokuttuk kāṭṭuvatu iyaṟkaiyōṭu avar evvaḷavu payiṉṟuḷḷār eṉpataṉaip pulappaṭuttum. “yāṉai veṇkōṭum akiliṉ kuppaiyum, māṉ mayirkkavariyum, matuviṉ kuṭaṅkaḷum, cantaṉak kuṟaiyum cinturakkaṭṭiyum, añcaṉat tiraḷum, aṇiyari tāramum, ēlavalliyum iruṅkaṟivalliyum, kūvai nūṟum, koḻuṅkoṭik kavalaiyum, teṅkiṉ paḻaṉum tēmāṅkaṉiyum, paiṅkoṭip paṭalaiyum, palaviṉ paḻaṅkaḷum, kāyamum karumpum pū malikoṭiyum, koḻuntāṭkamukiṉ ceḻuṅkulait tāṟum, peruṅkulai vāḻaiyiṉ iruṅkaṉittāṟum, āḷiyiṉ aṇaiṅkum, ariyiṉ kuruḷaiyum, vāḷvarip paṟaḻum, matakarik kaḷapamum, kuraṅkiṉ kuṭṭiyum kuṭāvaṭi yuḷiyamum, varaiyāṭu varuṭaiyum maṭamāṉ maṟiyum, kācaṟaik karuvum mācaṟu nakulamum, pīli maññaiyum nāviyiṉ piḷḷaiyum, kāṉakkōḻiyum tēmoḻikkiḷḷaiyum” ākiya ivaṟṟai malaivāṇar talaiyiṟ cumantu vantu vaittu “ēḻpiṟappaṭiyēm, vāḻka niṉ koṟṟam” eṉa vāḻtti, piṉpu, kaṇṇaki vāṉuṟṟa ceytiyaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
aimpulaṉkaḷukkum iṉpam viḷaippaṉavākiya malai paṭuporuḷ aṉaittaiyum, ōr kāṭci maṉṟattil tokuttu vaippatu pōl iṅkeṭuttiyampiyuḷḷa itu malaināṭṭu maṉṉaṉ maintarākiya avar malaiyiṉ iyaṟkai vaḷaṅkaḷai evvaḷavu naṉkaṟintuḷḷār eṉpataṉaip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu.
10.10.1941 aṉṟu tirucci vāṉoliyil pēciya pēccu.

21. kalittokai māṇpu
ulakiṉ kaṇ ellā uyirkaḷum iṉpattai viḻai kiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum, makkaḷoḻinta ēṉaiya uyirkaḷ yāvum uṇavum iṇaiviḻaiccum mutaliyavaṟṟāl eṉṟum oru peṟṟiyē ciṟucciṟu iṉpantuyttuvarutalaṉṟi, avviṉ pattaip perukkavum. ēṉait tuṉpattait tavirkkavum vakai yaṟiyātaṉavā yuḷḷaṉa. makkaḷ maṭṭumē tam pakuttaṟivu koṇṭu muyaṉṟu poruḷīṭṭiyum aṟam purintum, avai cārpāka ivvulakil nukarum iṉpattai mēṉmēṟ perukac ceytaṟkum, maṟumaiyiṉpiṉaiyum eṉṟum nilaiyāya vīṭṭiṉpiṉaiyum eytutaṟkum uriyarākiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē makkaḷāleytalākum pēṟukaḷ iṉpam,poruḷ, aṟam, vīṭu eṉṉum nāṉkumātal peṟṟām. īṇṭu iṉpam eṉṟatu immaiyiṉpamākum. in nāṉkiṉaiyum akam,puṟam eṉa iruvakaippaṭutturaippatu tamiḻ nūṉ muṟai. akapporuḷāvatu iṉpam. ēṉaiya puṟapporuḷ eṉappaṭum. 
im malartalai yulakil makkaḷ tuykkum iṉpam pala tiṟatta vāyiṉum, avai palavaṟṟuḷḷum, ulaka vaḷarccikkum, uyirkaḷiṉ malarccikkum iṉṟiyamaiyātatum, oru kālattoru poruḷāl aimpulaṉum nukartaṟ ciṟappuṭaiyatumākiya kāmaviṉpamē nūlōrāl iṉpameṉa vaittup pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. otta aṉpuṭaiya oruvaṉum oruttiyum kūṭukiṉṟa kālattup piṟanta pēriṉpam, akkūṭṭattiṉ piṉṉar oruvark koruvar pulaṉāka ivvāṟiruntateṉak kūṟappaṭātu yāṇṭum uḷḷattuṇarvē nukarntu iṉpamuṟuva toṉṟākaliṉ, aḵtu akapporuḷ eṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. ivviṉpattiṉai ettuṇaiyum viḻumi tākkutaṟkut tollai nallāciriyaṉmār kaṇṭa vittakat tiṟaṅkaḷ pala. avai aṉaittum ‘pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku’ eṉpatil aṭaṅkum. 
pulaṉeṟivaḻakkāvatu, ulakattu āṭavarum makaḷirum ulakiyalāṟ kūṭiyoḻukum ōḻukkattuṭaṉ nāṭaka vaḻakkāya puṉainturaiyum cērttup pulavarāyiṉār  ceppañ ceytamaittatōr tippiya neṟi. pulaneṟi vaḻakkilē otta aṉpuṭaiya talaivaṉun talaiviyuṅ kūṭiyoḻukum ōḻukkam ‘kaḷavu’ eṉavum ‘kaṟpu’ eṉavum iruvakaippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ kaḷavāvatu uruvum, tiruvum, paruvamum, kulaṉum, kuṇaṉum, aṉpum mutaliyavaṟṟāl otta talaimakaṉum, talaimakaḷum aṭuppāruṅ koṭuppārumiṉṟip pālvakaiyāl tāmē etirppaṭṭuk kūṭiyoḻukuvatu. kaṟpāvatu avviruvarum muṟaippaṭi varaintu koṇṭu illiliruntu aṟampurin toḻukuvatu. ivvirutiṟattum talaimakkaḷ ōḻukkam puṇartal, pirital, iruttal, iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉa aivakaippaṭṭu nikaḻumeṉpatum., avai muṟaiyē kuṟiñci, pālai, mullai, neytal, marutam eṉṉum aintiṇaikkum uriyavāmeṉpatum,. akattiṇaikaḷ mutal, karu, uri eṉap pākupāṭeytumeṉpatum, kaḷavoḻukkamāṉatu iyaṟkaippuṇarcci, iṭantalaippāṭu,. pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭameṉa nāl vakaiyāṉ nikaḻumeṉpatum, kaḷaviṉkaṇ talaivi tōḻikkum, tōḻi cevilikkum eṉṉum immuṟaiyāṉē aṟattoṭu nilai nikaḻumeṉpatum, iru kaikōḷiṉum kūṟṟu nikaḻttu taṟkuriyār iṉṉiṉṉār eṉpatum, avar iṉṉiṉṉa neṟip pāṭuṭaiyar eṉpatum, talaivaṟku .ivvivvētuvāṟ pirivu nikaḻum eṉpatum, avaṉ mīṇṭu varum poḻutum muṟaimaiyum ivvivai eṉpatum, piṟavum pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṭaik kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
‘iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuḷ’ uraiyāciriyar itaṉai ‘illatu iṉiyatu nallatu eṉṟu pulavarāl nāṭṭappaṭṭatō roḻukkam’ eṉṟē palaviṭattum vaṟpuṟuttic collā niṟpar. eṉiṉum ulakiyalum puṉainturaiyum viraviyatē pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkāmeṉal āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉāriṉ karutteṉpatu,
“	nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam”
eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. itaṉ uraiyil āciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar, “puṉainturai vakaiyiṟ kūṟupaveṉṟaliṉ pulavar illaṉavum kūṟupavālōveṉiṉ, ulakattōrkku naṉmai payattaṟku nallōrkkuḷḷaṉavaṟṟai oḻintōr aṟin toḻukal aṟameṉak karuti, annallōrkkuḷḷaṉavaṟṟiṟ ciṟitillaṉavum kūṟutaliṉṟi, yāṇṭum eññāṉṟum illaṉa kūṟār” eṉṟuraippatu cintikkaṟpālatu. 
ivaṟṟiliruntu, kaḷavuk kūṭṭameṉpatu tamiḻnāṭṭu aintiṇai makkaḷiṭam iyalpāka nikaḻntu vanta toṉṟeṉ patum, avarkaḷ mutaṟkaṇ kaḷavil aḷavaḷāviyiruntu piṉ varaintu koḷḷum oḻukalāṟuṭaiyar eṉpatum, ivaṟṟai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu nallicaippulavarkaḷ cuvai payappaṉavākiya karuttukkaḷ palavaṟṟait tokuttup pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṉai amaittuḷḷāreṉpatum karutaṟpālavākiṉṟaṉa. malaināṭṭu mutuvar mutalāyiṉārpāl iññāṉṟum kāṇappaṭum oḻukalāṟukaḷ ikkaruttiṉai valiyuṟuttuvaṉavākum. kaḷavuk kūṭṭam pulaṉeṟiyaṉṟi ulakiyalāṉum aintiṇai makkaḷiṭai nikaḻtaluṉṭeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉar nacciṉārkiṉiyareṉpatu,
“	mutaloṭu puṇarnta yāḻōr mēṉa tavalaruñ ciṟappi ṉainnilam peṟumē”
eṉṉum kaḷaviyaṟ cūttirattiṟku avaruraitta uraiyāl aṟiyappaṭum. malaivāṇariṭam ivvoḻukkam peru vaḻakka māyiṉamai koṇṭē tollāciriyaṉmār kuṟiñcikkaṇ puṇarcciyai amaittaṉar pōlum. eṉiṉum kūṭutal mutaliya uripporuḷkaṭku nilamum kālamum varaiyaṟai cey tiruppatil nāṭaka vaḻakku iyaintuḷḷamai tēṟṟam.
ivvāṟu pulaṉeṟiyiṟ puṉainturaikaḷ pukuttap paṭṭamaiyiṉ nōkkam talaimakkaḷiṉ akavuṇarcciyākiya aṉpiṉaip palavāṟum veḷippaṭuttutalē yātal vēṇṭum.
aintiṇai yoḻukkamāṉatu aṉpiṉ pālateṉpatu,
“aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇai maruṅkiṟ kāmak kūṭṭaṅ kāṇuṅ kālai”
eṉat tolkāppiyamum,
“aṉpi ṉai ntiṇai k kaḷaveṉap paṭuvatu”
eṉavum,
“	kaḷavu kaṟpeṉak kaṇṇiya īṇṭaiyōr uḷanika ḻaṉpiṉ uyarcci mēṉa”
eṉavum iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyalum kūṟutalāṟ pōtarum.
pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṭaik kāṇappaṭum talaimakkaḷiṉ viyakkattakka aṉpiṉ tiṟaṉum, talaiviyatu kaṟpiṉ tiṇmaiyum mutalāya aka vuṇarcci mēmpāṭellām tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷiṉ ulakiyalāṉa uṇarvoḻukkavuyarcciyiṉiṉṟum utittaṉavē yākum. pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṉai yātta tamiḻppulavarkaḷiṉ uḷḷattē malarnta avvuyar paṇpukaḷ ēṉait tamiḻ makkaḷ akattil mukiḻttila eṉal yāṅṅaṉam amaiyum? kaḷa voḻukka māṉatu āriya makkaḷiṉ eṇvakai maṉṟaluḷ oṉṟāya yāḻōr kūṭṭattai oppatu eṉak kūṟappaṭuvatu, iruvar tamiyarāy etirppaṭṭuk kūṭutalākiya oru puṭai oppumai karutiyē yākum. yāḻōr kūṭṭattil piṉ kaṟpu nikaḻtal orutalai yaṉṟu. kaḷavuk kūṭṭamō kaṟpiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai yuṭaiyatu. maṟṟum, pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟ palavāṟum pukaḻnturaittaṟkēṟṟa uḻuvalaṉpiṉ viḻumiya tiṟaṉellām kaḷavukkē ciṟappurimai yuṭaiyaṉa. ‘coṟpāl amutivaḷ yāṉ cuvaiyeṉṉa’ eṉṉum tirucciṟṟampalak kōvaic ceyyuḷum, ataṟkup pērāciriyar eḻutiya uraiyum avvaṉpiṉ ciṟappiṉai naṉku viḷakkutal kāṇka. makaḷirkku uyiriṉum nāṇ ciṟantateṉpatum ataṉiṉuṅ kaṟpuc ciṟanta teṉpatum kaḷaviyaliṉ teḷiporuḷāka uḷḷaṉa. ittakaiya nāṇuṅ kaṟpum pēṇu maṭavār iññāṉṟum uḷarātal kaṇkūṭākaliṉ ivai ulakiyalātal illeṉal cālātu.
“	kuṭimutaṟ cuṟṟamum kuṟṟiḷai yōrum aṭiyōr pāṅkum āyamum nīṅki nāṇamum maṭaṉum nallō rēttum pēṇiya kaṟpum peruntuṇai yāka eṉṉoṭu pōntīṅ keṉṟuyar kaḷainta poṉṉē koṭiyē puṉaipūṅ kōtāy nāṇiṉ pāvāy nī ṇila viḷakkē kaṟpiṉ koḻuntē poṟpiṉ celvi”
eṉṟu kōvalaṉ kūṟuvaṉa kaṇṇakiyiṉ uṇmaip peṇmai nalaṅkaḷē yaṉṟō? iṅṅaṉam kāntaruvattiṉum ciṟanta nalaṅkaḷōṭu tamiḻariṉ kaḷavu muṟai tikaḻntamaiyāṟṟāṉ itaṉait tamiḻakkē uriyateṉac cāṉṟōr palarum kūṟuvārāyiṉareṉka.
paripāṭalil kuṉṟampūtaṉār pāṭiya oṉpatāñ ceyyuḷil 
“	nāṉmaṟai virittu nallicai viḷakkum vāymoḻip pulavīr kēṇmiṉ”
eṉa nāṉmaṟaip pulavarai viḷittuk kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉaiyum, avaṟṟuḷ meyyuṟṟu aṟiyātār iruvar aṉpottup pāṉmai vakaiyāl tāmē meyyuṟṟuk kūṭum kātaṟkūṭṭamākiya kaḷavē ciṟanta teṉpataṉaiyuṅ kūṟi vantu, itaṉai vēṇṭum poruḷilakkaṇattaiyuṭaiya tamiḻai āyātārkku itaṉ ciṟappu viḷaṅkā teṉpār,
“taḷḷāp poruḷiyalpiṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ āyvantilār koḷḷārik kuṉṟu payaṉ”
eṉṟu kūṟi, itu tamiḻukkē uriyatāmāṟum, ataṉāl tamiḻ ciṟantatāmāṟum valiyuṟutti uraittiṭṭamai kāṇṭum.
maṟṟum, patumaiyōṭuṅ kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻtta veṇṇiya cīvakaṉ,
“	tēvar paṇṇiya tī toṭai yiṉcuvai mēvar teṉṟamiḻ meypporu ḷātaliṉ”
eṉa ataṉait teṉṟamiḻiṉ meypporuḷāmeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, curamañcariyum cīvakaṉum kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻttiṉamaiyai, “iṉṟamiḻiyaṟkaiyiṉpam nilaipeṟa neṟiyiṟṟuyttār” eṉṟum tiruttakkatēvar kūṟiyuḷḷamaiyum itu tamiḻiyalā meṉpataṉai valiyuṟuttāniṟkum. nacciṉārk kiṉiyar ivaṟṟaic cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷap paṭutaliṉ ‘ulakiyal vaḻakkāṉa kāntaruvam’eṉpar.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl aintiṇaip pāṟpaṭum aṉpiṉ viḷaivākiya akap poruḷiṉ ciṟappu iṉitu pulaṉākā niṟkum. āyiṉ ulakattil iruvariruvarāy iṉpan tuykkum ellā makkaḷum,
“	piṟappē kuṭimai yāṇmai yāṇṭō ṭuruvu niṟutta kāmavāyil niṟaiyē yaruḷē uṇarvoṭu tiruveṉa muṟaiyuṟak kiḷanta oppiṉatu vakaiyē.”
eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟiya oppumai palavumuṭaiyarāta lillai. aṭiyōrum viṉaiceyvārum mutalāyiṉār tamakkuriyaraṉṟip piṟar ēviya vaḻi oḻukuvōrātaliṉ avaratu aṉpiṉ nilaiyum. iṉpamum kuṟaipāṭuḷḷaṉavēyākum. ākaliṉ avaruṭaiya iṉpa oḻukkaṅkaḷ talaimaippāṭuṭaiya naṉ makkaḷiṉ aṉpoḻukkaṅkaḷōṭu oru taṉmaiyavākā eṉpatu paṟṟip paṇṭait tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷ avaṟṟiṟkuk kaikkiḷai, peruntiṇaiyeṉap peyar koṭuttu akap puṟameṉa orucār otukkivaippārāyiṉar. ēṉai naṉmakkaḷ pāl ulakiyalāl nikaḻum orucār kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaiyum uḷa. avaiyum akappuṟamē yākumeṉka.
iṉi, ittakaiya pulaṉeṟivaḻakkup paṟṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷē puṟapporuṭ ceyyuḷiṉum mikutiyākac caṅkattuc cāṉ ṟōrāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. puṟapporuḷ muyaṟciyil uḻantu vāṭṭamuṟum makkaḷ uḷḷattil iṉpunīr pāyccik kiḷarcciyai viḷaippataṟku iḵtu ēṟṟa karuviyātal karutip pōlum itaṉai mikuttup pāṭuvārāyiṉar! maṟṟum kāṭcip poruḷum karuttup poruḷumākiya iru vakaip poru ḷaiyum iṉitamaittuk kaṟpār neñcam kāmuṟac ceyyuḷ puṉaitaṟku iḵtu ēṟṟa peṟṟiyatātalum karutaṟpāṟṟu.
aintiṇaiyiṉ pākupāṭākiya mutal karu urip poruḷ kaḷil mutaṟporuḷākiya nilam poḻutukaḷum avaṟṟaic cārntu tōṉṟum karupporuḷākiya vilaṅku, puḷ, maram, pū mutalāyiṉavum, āṇṭu vāḻum makkaḷum avarkaḷ cey toḻil mutalāyiṉavum kāṭcip poruḷām. urip poruḷākiya puṇartal, pirital mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi akattē tōṉṟum uṇarcci vakaikaḷ karuttup poruḷām.
“	oppum uruvum veṟuppum eṉṟā kaṟpum ērum eḻilum eṉṟā cāyalum nāṇum maṭaṉum eṉṟā nōyum vēṭkaiyum nukarvum eṉṟāṅ kāvayiṉ varuuṅ kiḷavi yellām nāṭṭiya marapiṉ neñkoḷi ṉallatu kāṭṭa lākāp poruḷa veṉpa”
eṉṉum poruḷiyaṟ cūttirattuk kiḷantōtiya oppu mutaliyaṉavum, ‘ellām’ eṉpataṉāl urai yakattē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya oḷi, aḷi, kāytal, aṉpu, aḻukkāṟu, poṟai, niṟai ,aṟivu mutalāyiṉavum karuttup poruḷāmāṟu orka.
iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷ uraiyāciriyarum, “ulakattup poruḷtāṉ iraṇṭu vakaiyāṉ uṇarttappaṭum. uṇmai māttirai uṇarttip piḻampu uṇarttappaṭātaṉavum, uṇmaiyum piḻampum uṇarttappaṭuvaṉavumeṉa” eṉṟu kūṟi avaṟṟuḷ uṇmai uṇarttip piḻampu uṇarttappaṭātaṉa vaṟṟukkuk kāmam mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, ‘oppum uruvum’ eṉṉum cūttirattuk kūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟaiyumē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār. ivaiyellām piḻampuṇarttappaṭāta vāyiṉum nallicaip pulavar tām colluntiṟaṅkaḷāl ivaṟṟai nam neñcilē niṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. poṟikaḷāṟ kāṇṭaṟkuriyavākiya iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ tiṟaṅkaḷaiyum nuṉittaṟintu kūṟu vatilum, karuttup poruḷāya aṉpu mutaliya uṇarccikaḷai veḷippaṭuttuvatilum, caṅkap pulavarkaḷiṉ vittakak kavittiṟam ettuṇaiyum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatām.
palvēṟu kālattirunta pulavarkaḷ palarāl pāṭap paṭṭaṉavum kaṭaiccaṅkat tiṟuti nāḷiṟ cāṉṟōr cilarāl ovvoru muṟai paṟṟit tokukkappaṭṭaṉavum ākiya eṭṭut tokaiyuḷ oṉṟāyatu kalittokai. iḵtu akapporuḷāya pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkaiyē poruḷāka uṭaiyatu; muḻutuṅ kalippāvāl amaintatu. āciriyar nallantuvaṉārāl pālai, kuṟiñci, marutam, mullai, neytal eṉṉum immuṟaiyē tokuttu niṟuttappeṟṟatu. itil uḷḷa pālai mutaliya aintu pakutikaḷum muṟaiyē peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ, kapilar, marutaṉiḷa nākaṉār, nalluttaraṉ, nallantuvaṉār eṉpōrāṟ pāṭappeṟṟaṉa eṉpatu or veṇpāvāl aṟiyalāvatu. ivvaintu pakutikaḷilum pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyum, pāṇṭiyark kuriya pati, yāṟu, malaikaḷākiya kūṭal, vaiyai, potiyil eṉpaṉavum, piṟavum pārāṭṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ ivaṟṟai iyaṟṟiya āciriyar varalāṟu ārāyntu aṟutiyiṭap peṟal vēṇṭum. iṉi, kalittokaiyeṉṉum ivvilakkiyattiṉ ciṟappu aḷaviṭaṟkariya toṉṟākum. pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṉaip pāṭutaṟkuc ciṟappurimaiyuṭaiya ceyyuḷ kaliyē yeṉpatu.
“	nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam kaliyē paripāṭ ṭāyīru pāṅkiṉum uriya tāku meṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl aṟiyap paṭum. ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟik kaliyai muṟkūṟiyatu paripāṭṭiṉum atu ciṟantatu eṉpataṉaip pulappaṭuttum. kaliyuḷḷum ciṟappuṭaiya ottāḻicaik kaliyē palavākavum ēṉaiya cilavākavum ittokaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. iṉpam paṟṟiya ekkaḷippu allatu maṉaveḻuccikkuk kaliyiṉ tuḷḷalōcai porutta muṭaittākaliṉ āciriyar pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟku itaṉaiyē ciṟantateṉak koṇṭārātal vēṇṭum. maṟṟum taravu tāḻicai mutaliya kaliyuṟuppukkaḷ pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutaṟ kuriyār oruvar maṟṟoruvarkku oṉṟu kaṭṭuraikkumiṭattu akkaruttiṉait tokac colliyum tūvāta nīkki vakutturaittu valiyuṟuttiyum, muṭiviṉkaṇ payaṉāl nakac colliyum naṉṟi payattaṟ kēṟṟa vevvēṟu ōcaiyum amaippum uṭaiyavākat tikaḻtalum itaṉatu ciṟappurimaikku ētuvātal vēṇṭum. 
iṉi, ikkalic ceyyuḷellām caṅku tiraṇṭu muraṉṟeḻum ōcaipōlat taḻaintu iṉpam payakkum ōcai nala muṭaiyaṉavāka, avaṟṟil amaintuḷḷa poruḷvaḷamum, karuttu māṇpum aḷaviṭaṟ kariya ciṟappiṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.. ittokaiyiṉ kaṇ aintiṇaiyiṉ karupporuḷkaḷiṉ iyalkaḷum, āṇṭu vāḻum makkaḷiṉ peṟṟimaikaḷum kiḻiyil eḻutikkāṭṭiṉā loppa aḻakuṟatteḷintu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. itiluḷḷa uvamaṅ kaḷiṉ māṭci yoṉṟē itaṉai iṇaiyaṟṟa ciṟappiṉatākkum takutippāṭuṭaiyatu. āyiṉ, iṉpam nutali eḻunta itaṉkaṇ pālaiyiṉ koṭumai palapaṭiyākak kūṟappaṭutal tuṉpa niṉaivai yeḻuppi iṉpa veḻuccikku ūṟu ceyvatākumālō eṉiṉ, ākātu. eṉṉai? talaivaṉatu mīṭciyiṉkaṇ avviṉpam muṉṉaiyilum ciṟantu tōṉṟu mākaliṉ eṉka. allatūum talaimakaṉ taṉ kaṭappāṭukaḷai āṟṟutaṟkaṇ ettakaiya tuṉpaṅ kaḷaiyum poṟukku miyalpiṉaṉ eṉpataṉai nāṭṭutaṟkum, talaivaṉaip pirinta talaiviyiṉ talaiyaṉpu paṟṟiya aka uṇarccikaḷaik kāṭṭutaṟkum, talaivaṉ pirintu cellum pālaiyiṉ koṭumai kūṟal peritum payappāṭuṭaiyatātaluṅ kāṇka. talaiviyaip pirintu vēṟṟunāṭṭakalum talaiva ṉoruvaṉ yāṇṭum vemmai mikka āratarattam naṭantu cēṟal ulakiyalil orutalaiyaṉṟāyiṉum, muṟkuṟitta payaṉ karutiyē tollāciriyar nāṭaka vaḻakkup paṟṟi itaṉai amaittārākal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam iṟappa iṉṉātavaṟṟai uraikka vēṇṭiya iṭattum nallicaip pulavarkaḷ tam colluntiṟattāl avaṟṟiṟ kiṉimaiyūṭṭik kaṟpavar kāmuṟumāṟu ceytuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. 
iṉi, tūyatoru kātaliṉpam tuykkum ivaruḷ talaivaṉāṉavaṉ parattaiyiṟ pirintoḻukum oḻukkamum iḻivu tarum nīratākavē tōṉṟukiṟatu. iḵtulakiyal pōlap pulaṉāyiṉum nāṭaka vaḻakkup paṟṟiyē pulamai mikkār itaṉaip pukutturaittārātal vēṇṭum. 
iṉi, tūyatoru kātaliṉpam tuykkum iruvaruḷ talaivaṉāṉavaṉ parattaiyiṟ pirintoḻukum ōḻukkam matikkaṇ maṟuppōlva toṉṟāyt tōṉṟukiṟatu. iv voḻukkamāṉatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu pērurai yāciriyarkaḷiṉ uḷḷattait tikaikkac ceytuḷateṉpatu aṉṉōr paṉṉeṟippaṭa itaṟku amaiti kūṟuntiṟattāṉ aṟiyappaṭum.
“kātaṟ parattai ellārkku murittē”
eṉṉum iṟaiyaṉārakap poruṭ cūttira vuraiyil āciriyar nakkīraṉār parattaiyiṟ pirivu nikaḻtaṟku ētuvākak kūṟappaṭum pala karuttukkaḷai ārāyntu kaḻittup, puṉainturai vakaiyāl or karuttiṉai niṟuvi, iṟutiyil, “parattaiyiṟ pirintāṉ talaimakaṉ eṉṟāl ūṭalē pulaviyē tuṉiyē eṉṟivai nikaḻum, nikaḻntāl avai nīkkik kūṭiṉaviṭattum periya tōriṉpamāy avviṉpat taṉmaiyai veḷippaṭuppaṉa avaiyeṉak koḷka” eṉṟu muṭikkiṉṟaṉar. 
tirucciṟṟampalak kōvaiyāriṉ uraiyil pērāciriyar “parattaiyiṟ piriveṉpatu talaimakaḷai varainteytiya piṉṉar vaikalum pālē nukarvāṉoruvaṉ iṭaiyē puḷiṅkāṭiyum nukarntu ataṉ iṉimaiyai nukarntāṟpōla, avaḷ nukarcciyiṉ iṉimai yaṟitaṟkup puṟappeṇṭir māṭṭup piriyāniṟṟal allatūum paṇṇum pāṭalum mutalāyiṉa kāṭṭip puṟappeṇṭir taṉṉaik kātalittāl tāṉ ellārkkum talaivaṉātaliṉ avarkkum iṉpañ ceyyap piriyā niṟṟal eṉṟumām. allatūum talaimakaḷai ūṭalaṟi taṟkup pirital eṉṟumām” eṉṟu kūṟāniṟpar. ivaiyellām parattaiyiṟ piriveṉpatu nāṭaka vaḻakkup paṟṟi amaikkappaṭṭa teṉṉum karuttiṉai, uṭkoṇṭu, aṅṅaṉam amaikkappaṭu taṟku ētukkaḷai ārāynta vāṟēyām. varaiviṉ makaḷiroṭu maruviyoḻukum pūriya voḻukkam celvar cilariṭai ulakiyalāṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum, cīriyarākum talaimakkaḷ ataṉai uṭaiyarātal orutalaiyaṉṟē; ātalāṉ, cila payaṉ karutip pulavarāyiṉār itaṉaip puṉainturaittaṉareṉṟē kōṭal vēṇṭum. ap payaṉkaḷuḷ, talaivaṉ ettuṇaik koṭumai ceyyiṉum talaivi taṉ kaṟpiṉiṉṟum ciṟitum kalaṅkutal ilaḷāvaḷ eṉpataṉai veḷippaṭuppatē talaiyāyatākum. māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ kūṭaliṉāynta tīntamiḻk kaṭaliṟ paṭintu koḻitteṭutta arumpeṟaṉ maṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟāya,
“	vēyātu ceppi ṉaṭaittut tamivaikum vīyiṉaṉṉa tīyāṭi ciṟṟam palamaṉaiyāḷ tillai yūraṉukkiṉ ṟēyāp paḻiyeṉa nāṇiyeṉ kaṇṇiṅṅa ṉēmaṟaittāḷ yāyā miyalpivaḷ kaṟpunaṟ pāla viyalpukaḷē”
eṉṉum kōvaic ceyyuḷil, talaivaṉatu parattaimai yoḻukkattāl, vēyātu ceppiṉaṭaitta pūppōlat talaiviyiṉ mey vāṭiyum talaivaṉukku ovvāp paḻi varumeṉṟu añci, avaḷ, avaṉatu koṭumaiyait taṉ āruyirt tōḻikkum maṟaitta lākiya kaṟpiṉ poṟpu iṉitu viḷakkappaṭutal kāṇka,
iṉi, miṭāc coṉṟikku ōr aviḻpatamām eṉṉum muṟaiyāl kalittokaip poṟpēḻai yakattu oḷiviṭum katirmaṇik kuvaiyuḷ oru cila kāṭṭi ivvurai muṭikkappeṟum.
pālaik kaliyuḷḷē, curattiṉ vemmaiyaik kuṟittaṟku,
“	uṇṇīr vaṟappap pularvāṭu nāvikut taṇṇīr peṟāat taṭumāṟ ṟaruntuyaram kaṇṇīr naṉaikkum kaṭumaiya kāṭu”
eṉṟum,
“	iṉṉiḻaliṉmaiyāl varuntiya maṭappiṇaikkut taṉṉiḻalaik koṭuttaḷikkuṅ kalai”
eṉṟum, kūṟuvaṉa neñciṉai urukkun tiṟattaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉkaṇ avalamēyaṉṟi, aruḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa, piṉṉataṉ kaṇ, kāṭṭakattu vāḻum vilaṅkum ittakaiya pēraṉpiṉa tākaliṉ ataṉai yaṟintu talaiviyait talaiyaḷi ceytaṟkut talaivaṉ vārātirāṉ eṉṉum karuttu valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. 
“vīṟucāl ñālattu’ eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil, iḷavēṉiṟ kālattu yāṟu taṉṉālē vaḷampeṟṟa nāṭṭiṉ poṟpiṉaik kaṇviḻittuk kaṇṭāṟ pōl ataṉ maruṅkuḷḷa kayaṅkaḷ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, paḷikkuk kaṇṇāṭiyiṉuḷḷē pavaḻam patittāṟpōlak kāmpiṉiṉṟu kaḻaṉṟu utirnta murukkiṉ cēyitaḻkaḷ kuḷattu nīriṭaik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kūṟiya uvamaṅkaḷ maruṭkaiyum makiḻcciyum viḷaippaṉa.
kuṟiñcik kaliyilē ‘kāṉavaṉ yāṉai mītu vīciya kavaṇkal vēṅkaiyiṉ pūvaic citaṟi, āciṉi meṉpaḻattai yutirttu, tēṉiṉ iṟālait tuḷaippaṭuttu, māviṉ kulaiyai uḻakki, vāḻaiyiṉ maṭalaik kiḻittu, palaviṉ paḻattuṭ ceṉṟu taṅkum’ eṉak kuṟiñciyiṉ karupporuḷ palavaṟṟaicciṟappu ciṟappuṟa eṭuttaṭukki,avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ akapporuḷ oḻukka nikaḻccikaḷai uḷḷuṟut tamaittirukkum tiṟappāṭu pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu.
marutak kaliyil,
“	kātalkoḷ vatuvaināṭ kaliṅkattuḷ oṭuṅkiya mātarkoḷ māṉōkkiṉ maṭantaitaṉ tuṇaiyāka ōtuṭai yantaṇaṉ erivalañ ceyvāṉpōl”
aṉṉam taṉ aṇinaṭaip peṭaiyōṭu tāmarait taṉi malaraip puṟañcūḻntu tiritarum eṉṟuraippatu iṉimai payappatoṉṟām.
mullaik kaliyilē, veḷḷai, kāri, kurāl, cēy, kari, neṟṟik kāri, cemmaṟu veḷḷai, ēntimiṟ kurāl, palpoṟip pukar eṉṟiṅṅaṉam niṟampaṟṟi ēṟukaḷukkup peyar kūṟi, avaṟṟukkup palarāmaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, mukkaṇṇāṉ, murukaṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷarkaḷaiyum, avarkaḷ tarittuḷḷa tār, vaḷai, maṟu, uṭai mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum poruntavaittu uvamaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu pārāṭṭun tiṟattatu.
“	kollēṟṟuk kōṭañcuvāṇai maṟumaiyum pullāḷē āya makaḷ”
eṉpataṉ karuttu aṟintu cuvaittu iṉpuṟaṟpālatu.
neytaṟkaliyuḷ, talaivaṉaip pirintu āṟṟāmai mikkuk kaṭal, mukil, ñāyiṟu mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkit talaivi kūṟum kaḻipaṭark kiḷaviyellām iruppu neñcaiyum nekiḻkkun tiṟattaṉa.  āṟṟutal, pōṟṟutal, paṇpu, aṉpu, aṟivu, ceṟivu, niṟai, muṟai, poṟai eṉṉum paṇpukaḷai oru ceyyuḷil eṭuttu niṟutti, “āṟṟutal eṉpatoṉ ṟalantavark kutavutal” eṉṟiṅṅaṉam avaṟṟiṉ karuttiṉai varaiyaṟut tuṇartti yiruppatu āciriyaratu māṇ pulamaikkuc cāṉṟākum.
“	tampukaḻ kēṭṭārpōl talaicāyttu marantuñca” 
eṉṟu āciriyar uvamamākat tērntu kūṟiyatōr naṟpaṇpu naṉmakkaḷ uḷḷattē eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavutaṟ kuriyatu.
maṟṟum ikkalittokaiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭirukkum talai makkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷum, aṟaneṟikaḷum, ulakiyalkaḷum mutalāyiṉa vellām pēruṟuti payappaṉavākum. mutal karuurip porukaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷait teḷivuṟukku miṭaṅkaḷil vēttiyal mutalāya ēttarum aṟaneṟikaḷ ettaṉaiyō pala uvamaṅkaḷil vaittu aṟivuṟuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. makkaḷiṉ maṉanilai, oḻukkam mutaliya paṇpukaḷukku maram, pū mutaliya parupporuḷ kaḷaiyum, parupporuḷkaḷukku appaṇpukaḷaiyum uvamaiyāka amaittirukkum aḻaku itaṉiṟpōla ittuṇai mikutiyāka vēṟeṅkum kāṇṭalaritu. itaṉaik kaṟpatil tam neñciṉait tiṟai koṭuttōrē tamiḻiṉ cuvaiyai iṉitu nukarntu iṉpuṟuvōrāvar.
kalittokaikku āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya urai viḻumiyatoṉṟu. avariyaṟṟiya urai palavaṟṟiṉum itu ciṟantatu eṉṉalām. nuṇmāṇ karuttukkaḷai āṇṭāṇṭu āyntu veḷippaṭuttiyum, uḷḷuṟaiyuvamaṅkaḷait teḷḷitiṉ viḷakkiyum, ceyyuḷtōṟum meyppāṭum, payaṉum terivittum ivvurai iyaṉṟuḷḷatu. cila toṭarkaḷukku veḷippaṭai yākat teriyum poruḷai viṭuttu, viḻumiya poruḷ pukutturaippatu ivvuraiyāciriyar iyalpeṉpatu palarum aṟintatoṉṟu. kaliyiṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttiluḷḷa,
“ kūṟāmaṟ kuṟittaṉmēṟ cellum kaṭuṅkūḷi  māṟāppōr maṇimiṭaṟ ṟeṇkaiyāy kēḷiṉi”
eṉpatil ‘kūṟāmaṟ kuṟittataṉmēṟ cellum’ eṉpatu kūḷikku aṭaimoḻiyāy niṉṟu, ‘kūṟavēṇṭāmalē karutiya viṉaiyiṟ cellum’ eṉṉum poruḷ payappatu kaṇkūṭu. takkayākap paraṇi uraiyāciriyar, “iṟaivartam, mēlai neṟṟi viḻikkavantu paṇintu niṉṟaṉaṉ vīraṉē” eṉpataṟku urai kūṟumiṭattu, “kūṟāmaṟ ... kēḷiṉi” eṉṉum ippakutiyai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruppatu avar karuttum aṉṉatātalaip pulappaṭuttum. āyiṉ, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarō, ataṉai iṟaivaṉukku aṭaiyākki, “māṟṟam maṉam kaḻiya niṉṟavaṉ” eṉṟa karuttiṉaip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟār. 

1940 ām āṇṭu mārccut tiṅkaḷ 9, 10 nāṭkaḷil ceṉṉaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa kalittokai  mānāṭṭil tamiḻaṟiñar na.mu.vē. avarkaḷ  nikaḻttiya talaimai urai.
